# Current milestone — 2026-11-27

100 of 270 Chemistry Labs passed internal functional review. CL-0100 native left drag and wheel were confirmed after the Mac was unlocked; screenshots and unchanged-reading/time evidence are in its private folder. CL-0101–0107 remain in progress in this publishing snapshot. Newer native evidence for those labs exists in the working checkout and has not yet been folded into their final review records.

The publishing snapshot passed all nine gates, including 3,315 tests, preserved source hashes for all 100 reviewed labs and a Pages-base production build. The workspace contains additional unrelated staged work; its 146 original staged blobs must remain untouched. The next scheduled batch boundary is 125 reviewed labs. The goal remains active.

Earlier checkpoints below are historical and superseded where inconsistent.

Exact inputs: initialWaterG80–120,heaterW40–300(delivered thermal power,not electrical draw),heatLossWK0–1.5,coolantFlowGS0–3,coolantInC10–25,targetG10–60,timeSeconds0–2400. Defaults100g/160W/UA.5/1.5g/s coolant20C/target50g. Pure reference water; no contaminant/potability or salt solubility solver. Ambient20C,boiling100C at101.42kPa reference;cp4.18J/(gK),latent2256.4J/g,vesselheatcapacity40J/K,model heater cutoff retains10g source liquid. These are stated approximations, not calibrated apparatus/physical safety specifications.

C0=m0cp+Cv; before boiling C0*Tdot=P−UA(T−20). Analytical exponential warming, UA0 linear. Finite boiling only P>UA*80; otherwise boilSeconds/heaterStopSeconds/targetSeconds NULL, not Infinity/fake success. Boiling rate=(P−UA80)/L, source evaporation cappedm0−10, heater automatically stops, remaining liquid/vessel then cool exponentially with sameUA; UA0 insulated hold. Coolant ideal latent capacity=mdot cp(35−Tin)W; capture=min(1,Qcap/Pnet); recovered=capture*evap; escaped=rest. Condensate stays HOT100C in ideal insulated collector; do not falsely show cold output/subcooling without energy term. Pump interlocked to boiling; cumulative throughput external to feed-water balance. Current coolant outlet is NULL when flow off; cumulative mean outlet may remain. Zero cooling means no capture, not false condensed product. A chosen low heater/largeUA can never boil. Target time only if capture-limited maximum permits it; distinguish current mass from future target prediction/within-run feasibility.

Heat/enthalpy budget relative20C: source sensible=(mremaining cp+Cv)(T−20); recovered sensible=mrec cp80; escaped enthalpy=mesc(cp80+L); coolant heat=mrec L; ambientloss includes analytical warm loss, UA80*boilingactive and post-cutoff sensible cooling. Sum equals supplied heat P*tactive. Primary feed remaining+recovered+escaped=m0, external coolant never counted as recovered feed. More heat can exceed the same condenser capacity and reduce total capture. Once-through coolant demand may be far larger than collected feed; collectionMinusDiscardedCoolantG is a CONDITIONAL operational comparison if coolant discarded, not water destruction. noPhaseChangeEquilibriumC is the unconstrained heating-ODE reference, not a claim that boiling source exceeds100C.

Volumes use linear interpolation of saturated-liquid reference densities.99816g/cm³ at20C and.95835at100C. Not fullEOS/pressure-dependent expansion. Recovered volume uses hot density. External exchanger is quasi-steady/zero hold-up, negligible thermal capacity; do not add arbitrary liquid/opaque steam/bubble inventories. Guide120s=10setup+100continuous physical0–2400(24x)+10hold; default has>60presentation seconds of boiling/collection. Need original geometry/record engine with Q=mcΔT, Pt, mL, loss/capture/target/energy/coolant worked substitutions; original2D/3D/UI then actual playback/records/independent responsive/camera/storage/navigation review beforefreeze. ## CL-0107 core, geometry and material asset — 2026-11-27
